### Fix Design (pre-decided; product rules as Lionel answered)

The SM prototype v1.5, on `097a12c`, implements all of it (`proto_1019.diff`; Reproduction Evidence v1.5); it is a measurement file. The dev agent writes the real code from this text.

**AC1, the forms rule: 10.17's functions, moved (`validation/models.py`; no `gui/` import, no Qt).** Since v1.5 (10.17 merged as `097a12c`), 10.19 writes no forms function of its own.
1. **The hand-over (Task 2.8; the PO's v1.2 S1).** 10.17's `fold`, `is_form` and `whole_word_places` (`gui/models/validation_state.py:65-136` at `097a12c`) move unchanged into `validation/models.py`, which already holds the boundaries they use (`_NOT_AFTER_WORD`, `_NOT_BEFORE_WORD`, the same `utils/text_form` pattern as 10.17's `not_after_word() + … + not_before_word()`). `validation_state.py` imports back the two it uses (`is_form`, `whole_word_places`; its own `re`, `unicodedata` and `utils.text_form` imports go when nothing else uses them). No other module and no test imports these names from `validation_state.py` (Code Map), and 10.17's and 10.20's tests stay unchanged and green (measured on the SM's prototype v1.5).
2. **What they do** (10.17's text, unchanged): `fold(text)` sets each character's accents aside on its own (Unicode NFD without the combining marks; a character whose result is not one character is kept), so positions are kept; `is_form(place, text)`: `place == text` is a form; a place with no capital letter is not; the same letters in small letters (`place.lower() == text.lower()`) is a form; a place with a small letter is not; else (Lionel's Q3, a place entirely in capitals) `fold(place).lower() == fold(text).lower()`; `whole_word_places(text, document_text)`: every whole-word form of `text`, searched case-insensitively on the folded text, left to right, not overlapping, each kept only if `is_form` holds for the text written there; an empty text has no place.
3. **Composed text (10.20).** Every text these functions see is composed: the readers (`read_file` and the others), the typed text (`get_text_input`) and the detector's spans all come from composed text, so a document whose accents are stored as separate marks behaves like the usual one (T19). 10.19 adds no normalization of its own.

**AC2, [A] (`validation/models.py`).**
1. `places_of(text, entity_type)`: the same units (each cell, or the text) and the same `DetectedEntity` fields (the text as typed, `source=ADDED_BY_HAND_SOURCE`, the cell), at every `whole_word_places(text, unit)`.
2. `is_listed_place(place)`: the found text is a form of the typed text (`is_form(review.entity.text, place.text)`), in place of the exact equality; the type, the span and the cell as today. A found « ZORBAL », or « HELENE » for an added « Hélène », at the place's span is then the same place. v1.15 (ADD-1019-01): either way round, `is_form(review.entity.text, place.text) or is_form(place.text, review.entity.text)`, or the text written at the place a form of the review's text; in the desktop, `_new_places` leaves out a place a row added by hand holds when its name and the typed text are forms of each other either way round, or the text written there is a form of its name.
3. `_handle_add_entity` (`workflow.py`) does not change: its "not found", "Added …" and approved lines follow from the places.

**AC3, the correction locating (`validation/workflow.py`).**
1. `_locate_modified_span(document_text, entity, new_text)`: the window and the exact search as today; when the exact search (with 10.20's check that a place does not part a letter from its accent) returns nothing, the first `(start, end)` of `whole_word_places(new_text, document_text[window_start - 1 : window_end + 1])`, offsets added back (v1.10, QA's PERF-001: the window only, one character more on each side for the word bounds), with `window_start <= start`, `end <= window_end` and an overlap with the old span; else the old span. v1.10: that search is `_reached_span` (the span or `None`); `_locate_modified_span` returns it, or the old span. v1.13 (Lionel's answer): when it is `None`, the old span is widened over the words the correction adds after the words it already holds, when the words written right after it are those words, accents set aside, with a capital (a helper `_widened_over_added_words`); else the old span. v1.15: each written word holds a capital and is a Q3 form of the typed word, or the typed word of it (`is_form` either way round, in place of the accents set aside), and the words are read across any whitespace (`_NEXT_WORD`: `\s+`, a line break included). v1.17 (LEAK-1019-02): `_NEXT_WORD` also ends a word at « “ », « ” » and « „ », and a closing « ' » or « ’ » at the end of the word read is taken off it (`rstrip`) before the comparison, the span ending before it; an apostrophe inside a word stays. v1.19: `_NEXT_WORD` and the closing marks go; a helper `_next_word(text, position)` reads, after at least one whitespace character, a letter, then letters and accent marks (`is_mark`), a hyphen (`-`, U+2010, U+2011) or an apostrophe (« ' », « ’ ») only when a letter follows it; anything else ends the word. v1.21: `_next_word` gives way to `_word_end(text, at)` (the same word, except that a hyphen right after a single letter ends it: « J.-M. » and « J-M » are two initials, « d'Émerine » stays one word), `_words(text, start, end)` (every word of a text by that rule) and `_word_after(text, position)` (the next word, past whatever is not a letter). `_widened_over_added_words` reads the held words (`_words` over the span) and the typed ones (`_words` over the correction); the held words start the correction (fold, small letters); each added word is `_word_after` the one before, with a capital and a Q3 form either way (rulings 1 and 2), and `_between(text, word, end)` is the same in the document and in the correction: the characters between the two words, each run of whitespace read as one space, a full stop right after a word of one letter set aside. The span ends after the last matched word. Joined groups (sent back) and the desktop's rows of another name (`_reaches`) do not use it. One import of `whole_word_places`. A form never parts a letter from its accent: its boundaries hold combining marks.
2. The callers do not change (CLI [E], the desktop single-document review).

**AC3, desktop batch review (Lionel's Q4; `gui/models/validation_state.py`).**
1. `_action_rows(review)`: for every other row, its `_name_key` has the same type as the review's and its text is a form of the review's (`is_form(other_text, text)`), in place of the exact `_name_key` equality, and, only when the two `_name_key`s differ (a row reached through the forms rule; the orchestrator's rulings on the PO's v1.2 and v1.3 M1), the text written at every place that row stands for (v1.8: `self._session.text_of(place)[place.start_pos:place.end_pos]`, not `place.text`, which for a place added by hand is the name as typed; the orchestrator's ruling, 2026-10-25) is a form of the review's name: 10.17's `self._occurrences(other)` (`:833-847`: a row added by hand's places, or a detected row's variant group), else `[other.entity]`. A row with exactly the same `_name_key` is reached as today (10.10), whatever places it stands for; a row rejected on its own is skipped, as today (`_decided_alone(other, _DROPPED)`). `is_form` comes from `validation.models` (Task 2.8). v1.13: when the review row has a text edit (`review.user_modification`), another row holding the same edit is also reached when the text at every place it stands for, read at the span its edit gave it (`_edited_spans_by_id`, else its own span; a helper `_current_places`), is a form of that edit. v1.15 (CAPS-004): a row holding the same edit is taken before the original-name gate (`is_form(other_name[0], text)`), with no text check: `modify_entity_text`'s `_reaches` below decides, from `_current_places`. `modify_entity_text` then keeps a row whose `_name_key` differs from the review's only when `ValidationWorkflow._reached_span` finds the corrected text, or a capitals form of it (not the widening below), at every one of its places from those spans (a helper `_reaches`); the others are left as they are. A type change reaches the rows of the first point.
2. `_group_ids`, `_pseudonym_rows`, `accept_entity`, `reject_entity` do not change. `modify_entity_text` and `change_entity_type` call `_action_rows` as today. The same code serves the single-document review, where « Vorlène » and « VORLENE » are two rows (T15), and where a row standing for « VORLENE » and « Vorlene » is not reached (G8), while a row added by hand still reaches the detected row of exactly its name and type, as today (G9). Batch rows have one place each, so batch is unchanged.

**AC3, the CLI review's groups (the PO's M2; `validation/models.py`).**
1. `ValidationSession.get_entity_groups(entity_type)`: after today's variant grouping (`group_entity_variants`, unchanged), the groups are taken by their first place, left to right, and a group joins the first kept group of the same type when every text written at a place of one (v1.8: `self.text_of(entity)[entity.start_pos:entity.end_pos]` for each occurrence, not the key and variant texts, since a place added with [A] carries the name as typed) is a form of a text written at a place of the other, either way round. v1.10 (Codex P0-1; the orchestrator's ruling): every text of the group is a form, either way round, of every text already in the joined group, `all(is_form(a, b) or is_form(b, a) for a in group for b in joined)`, and the joined texts grow with each join; so a join never chains (the PO's v1.2 M1 and Codex's case both), and `_all_forms` goes. The joined group keeps the first group's key and canonical entity, takes both groups' places in the order of the session's entities, and the union of their variant texts. Groups of other types, names that are not forms (« Vorlene » for « Vorlène », G7), and a group holding a form and another name (today's « VORLENE » with « Vorlene », G8) stay apart.
2. Its callers do not change: the per-type review (`workflow.py:255`), [E] on the group (each place through `_locate_modified_span`, which reaches « VORLENE QUENTRIX » by the forms, AC3; v1.10: item 3), the summary's unique count (`workflow.py:230`, `models.py:625`) and `names_left_in_clear` (`models.py:597`). `nlp/entity_grouping.py` does not change (AC10).
3. **v1.10, a place the correction does not reach (QA's CAPS-001; the orchestrator's ruling).** `EntityGroup` gets `joined_parts: list[list[DetectedEntity]]` (default empty): the places each join brought in, one list per group joined. [E] (`workflow.py:335-362`) calls a new `ValidationWorkflow._apply_correction(session, group, new_text) -> list[EntityGroup]`: a group with no join, every place as today; a joined group, every place through `_reached_span`, and a place it finds nothing for is not modified: the group's own such places, and each joined part's, become a new group each (key and canonical entity its first place), returned in order and inserted right after the current group in the review loop.

**AC4, the erasure (`data/repositories/mapping_repository.py`, `cli/commands/delete_mapping.py`).**
1. Two methods, abstract in `MappingRepository` and implemented in `SQLiteMappingRepository`: `find_all_by_full_name_any_capitals(full_name) -> list[Entity]`: first the rows `_query_by_full_name` finds for the name (10.20: every form an earlier version saved it in, every type, in its order), then every other saved pseudonym, decrypted as `find_all` does, whose name, composed (10.20's `composed`), is the same whatever its capitals (`stored.lower() == full_name.lower()`) or is a form of the name (`is_form`: written entirely in capitals with its letters once accents are set aside), or (the PO's v1.5 M1; Lionel's 10.20 Qa; v1.10, Codex P0-2) whose name, composed, is the same letters as, or `is_form` of, one of `saved_name_forms(name, type)` composed, with the LOCATION forms for a LOCATION row and the PERSON forms for every other type, as `_query_by_full_name` builds them (`_FORM_TYPES`, `_name_forms`); a form that lost letters (not every word of the name, accents set aside, is among its words in order) matches only a row whose saved text carries an accent mark (`is_mark`), so the sign is kept; a row is checked only when its composed text, accents set aside, in small letters, is that of the name or of one of the forms (one `fold` per row); v1.13: before that comparison and that fold, every accent mark of the saved text and of the forms is written as one mark (U+0301; a helper `_one_mark`), so a substituted accent v2.2.0 left alone still meets the form; the sign rule reads the saved text as it is; and (QA's second shape) the forms that lost letters of the name in capitals, accents set aside, with an accent put back on each letter where v2.2.0's title step could cut (a vowel that ends a title word at the start of a word, or that a title word follows to the end of the word; every choice of them, at most six; a helper `_damage_seeds`), each reaching only a row written entirely in capitals that carries a mark; v1.15 (DEL-1019-02, QA's OM4): `_one_mark` only in that capitals-only branch, the name's own old forms compared with their marks as they are (`_same_or_form(text, form)`); (DEL-1019-03) `_damage_seeds` reads each part of a word between hyphens and apostrophes as a word (a helper `_word_parts`); v1.17 (DEL-1019-04): the seeds branch compares keys, `_seed_key(text)`: the text decomposed, a letter's own accent (the first mark after it) set aside, every other mark written U+0301, in small letters; a row in capitals with a mark whose `_seed_key` is among the keys of the lost-letter forms goes; (DEL-1019-05) `_MAX_DAMAGE_SPOTS = 8`, from the data (the app's name lists: at most one place per word, so 4 for a four-word name; twice that); v1.19 (DEL-1019-06): `_damage_seeds` and the bound go. For each row in capitals with a mark, `_cut_sets(words, letters, location)` walks the name (in capitals, accents set aside) and the row's letters (decomposed, marks taken off) once, memoised on the two positions: at each place where v2.2.0 could cut (`_damage_spots`: the vowel ending a title word at a part's start, or followed by a title word to the part's end, parts between hyphens and apostrophes), the title word is taken off (with the spaces after it, for a word's end) or kept, and a « MAITRE » word or a place's leading « A », which 10.20's replay inserts, may be skipped in the row; it gives every set of cut places that turns the name's letters into the row's. For each set, the name with an accent put back at those places is replayed (`_name_forms`, as the seeds were), and the row goes when its `_seed_key` is among the forms that lost letters; the sets' forms are kept for the call; v1.21 (DEL-1019-07): `_cut_sets` and `_damage_spots` go for `_cut_places(words, row, location, titles)`, a walk over the name (in capitals, accents set aside, its words joined by one space) and the row (decomposed, in capitals, spaces collapsed, its marks kept), memoised on the two positions: a letter's own mark is skipped; at a mark that starts a word, follows a separator or follows another mark, any title word of the name starting there may have been taken off, its last letter accented (and the letter before, when the mark before is that letter's own accent); after a mark that follows a letter, a title word ending a part may have been taken off with the spaces after it, the letter before it accented; letters match one by one; 10.20's inserted « MAITRE », and « A » at a place's start, may be skipped in the row. Each set of offsets it gives is replayed as before (`_name_forms`, `_seed_key`); v1.23 (DEL-1019-08): the walk's branches give way to four moves, and the old step itself decides: `_name_forms` replays v2.2.0's title step (global, again until nothing changes) on the name accented at each set, and the row goes only when that replay gives it, as before. At each pair of positions the walk may match a letter; read a mark of the row as the accent of the name's letter just read, when that letter is in `_MARKED_LETTERS = frozenset("AEIOUYC")` (the letters French writes with an accent); take off a title word of the name starting there, with or without the spaces after it, where a bound can stand before it (the name's start or the row's, after a separator, a space or a mark, a mark a cut left alone included); or pass over 10.20's inserted « MAITRE », or « A » at a place's start. So every sequence of cuts the old step can make is offered, and only the replay keeps one; v1.25 (the eighth Codex review's P0): passing over an inserted word takes the cut's own predicate, `bound_before(i, j)` (the name's start or the row's, after a separator, a space or a mark), in place of « the row's start or after a space », and a place's « A » is offered wherever the title is; at the row's end, after the name's last letter, a space and an inserted word may close the row (the space before it is its bound); and `reaches_old_row` replays every set the walk gives, the empty one included (the name's capitals with every accent set aside, a Q3 form like the others), in place of the non-empty ones only; v1.27 (the ninth Codex review's P0, QA's DEL-1019-10): `bound_before(i, j)` is the old title pattern's own bound, read from it: `_TITLE_BOUND`, the part of `FRENCH_TITLE_PATTERN` before its first « (?: » (« \b »), and `_bound_after(char)`, whether that bound holds between `char` and a title word's first letter; the bound stands at the name's start or the row's, or after a character of the name or of the row for which `_bound_after` holds (a space, a hyphen, a mark, « ( », « [ », « / », a quotation mark...); `_SEPARATORS` goes. `_lone_mark(text)`: a mark at the start or right after a character, not a mark, for which `_bound_after` holds. `lost_forms` keeps, besides `_name_forms`' forms, the old step's own keys of the seed (`strip_french_titles` on each of 10.20's `_key_texts(lookup_key(seed, type), type)`) that hold a lone mark: 10.20's sign read with the same bound. At the row's end only « MAITRE » is passed over (the place's « A » there goes); by name and type and `delete_entities_by_full_name_any_capitals(full_name) -> list[Entity]` (those deleted in one transaction, rolled back on `OperationalError` as `delete_entities_by_full_name` does; the decrypted ones returned). Every saved row is decrypted once for the first method (as 10.20's `search_entities` does; the cost is in Reproduction Evidence v1.6). The accents test is AC1's `is_form`, imported from `validation.models` inside the method (the data layer must not import `gui/`; the core already imports `validation.models`). `find_all_by_full_name` and `delete_entities_by_full_name` do not change (the core uses the first, `document_processor.py:762`).
2. `delete_mapping_command` by name (composed by 10.20, `:88-91`): the two new methods at `:135` and `:165`; the ERASURE loop and the success lines as they are (each deleted saved pseudonym its entry and its success lines, also with `--force`). `--id` unchanged except its line (AC15). The module docstring (`:3-7`) and `delete_mapping_command`'s (`:68-70`, « one per type it is stored under ») follow the new rule (the PO's v1.2 N1): by name, every saved pseudonym of the name, whatever its type or its capitals (capitals without accents included); by `--id`, one, and the others of the same name written exactly the same way are reported.

Deleting by name lists all of them, then deletes them all (each deletion is logged as an ERASURE entry); `--id` deletes only one and tells you which others remain for the same name written exactly the same way. »

La suppression par nom les affiche tous, puis les supprime tous (chaque suppression est consignée par une entrée ERASURE) ; `--id` n'en supprime qu'un et indique ceux qui restent pour le même nom écrit exactement de la même façon. »

The types are those of the saved pseudonyms left, in the repository's order (by type), as today.

**What the user sees:** after [A] « Zorbal », « ZORBAL » is written with Zorbal's pseudonym, and « 'Zorbal' is already in the review as PERSON at every place: nothing added » no longer shows while a form is left; after [A] « Hélène », « HÉLÈNE » and « HELENE » too; a correction reaches its capital places, in the desktop reviews its capital rows too, and the CLI review shows « Vorlène » and « VORLENE » as one name; `delete-mapping Zorbal` lists « Zorbal », « ZORBAL » (and any other capitals, or capitals without accents) before the confirmation, with Lionel's line, and erases them all, each with its entry; after `delete-mapping --id`, « 1 other mapping with the same name remains (type: ORG). ».

### Test Plan

Two new files in `tests/unit/`, written by the BMAD dev agent (Claude): `test_cli_capitals_forms.py` (T1-T7, T9-T13, T17, T18, T19 CLI, T20, G1, G2, G7, G8 CLI) drives the real `gdpr-pseudo process` and `delete-mapping` commands; `gui/test_capitals_correction_desktop.py` (T8, T14, T15, T16, T19 desktop, G3, G4, G5, G8 desktop, G9) drives the real desktop single-document and batch reviews. Invented names only; a database per test (`init_database`, passphrase `zorbalia_pass_A_123!` from `GDPR_PSEUDO_PASSPHRASE`); no spaCy model. **Preconditions are `pytest.fail("PRE: …")` calls** (also in the helpers), never `assert`; the first `assert` after them is the expected failure on the unfixed code. The tests assert the written files, the stored rows, the lines shown and the ERASURE entries.

**Harness (CLI)**, as 10.15's committed `tests/unit/test_cli_corrections_hand_added.py`: `typer.testing.CliRunner().invoke(app, ["process", <input>, "--db", <db>, "-o", <output>], env={"GDPR_PSEUDO_PASSPHRASE": …}, catch_exceptions=False)`; `readchar.readkey` from a list (Enter passes the summary screen; Space after the list ends); `rich.prompt.Prompt.ask` from a list (typed texts, and « yes » for `delete-mapping`'s confirmation); `rich.prompt.Confirm.ask` returns True; detection replaced on `DocumentProcessor._get_detector` and `_detect_and_filter_entities` by a fake that finds every occurrence of each (name, type) given, with its exact capitals (`first_only`: only its first occurrence); settings: `HOME`, `USERPROFILE` and the current folder at `tmp_path`, `gui.config._config_path` into it. Rows: `open_database` + `SQLiteMappingRepository.find_all()`, read as (`full_name`, `entity_type`, `pseudonym_full`). ERASURE entries: the `operations` table read with `sqlite3`, `operation_type = 'ERASURE'`, its `user_modifications` JSON. Rows for T9 and G2 are stored first through `DocumentProcessor.finalize_document` (a precondition).
**Harness (desktop)**, as 10.17's and 10.10's desktop tests. Single document: `DetectionWorker` run synchronously with `DocumentProcessor.detect_entities` patched to return the text and the entities given; `ValidationScreen.start_validation`; the correction through `GUIValidationState.modify_entity_text(<the row's id>, <text>, <start>, <end>)`; every pending row accepted; `_on_finalize`; wait until the results screen; the written text read from its temporary output. Batch: `BatchScreen` with validation, one `.txt` document, `DocumentProcessor._detect_and_filter_entities` patched, the batch worker thread waiting for the review, the decisions, then the finalize button; the written `<stem>_pseudonymized.txt`. Rows as above. Every `ConfirmDialog` answers yes. **T19's desktop half reads the file:** the real `DetectionWorker` → `DocumentProcessor.detect_entities` → `read_file` (10.20: composed), with only `DocumentProcessor._detect_and_filter_entities` replaced (the fake's places on the text it is given). **Screen texts** are compared with their whitespace collapsed (`" ".join(output.split())`), since Rich wraps long lines at the console width.

**Strings.** Invented: « Zorbal », « ZORBAL », « zorbal », « Zorbal Quentrix », « ZORBAL QUENTRIX », « zorbal quentrix », « Vorlan Mestrix », « Vorlan Zorbal », « Vorlan ZORBAL », « Vorlan de Zorbal », « VORLAN DE ZORBAL », « SAINT-ZORBAL », « Zorbalix », « Vorlène », « VORLÈNE », « VORLENE », « Vorlene », « Vorlène Quentrix », « VORLENE QUENTRIX », « Vorlene Quentrix », « Quovôme Zorbal », « QUOVÔME ZORBAL » (and its old saved text « QUOVO » + U+0302 + « ZORBAL »); the sheet's header « Nom », « Note » and « ok ». Real words, all in the main corpus (`scripts/accuracy_heldout_blind_check.py`, `checked=17 not_in_main_corpus=0`): valide, Le, contrat, avec, Signé, a, signé, et, de, petit, Petit, PETIT, ok, Nom, Note, SAINT, Saint. Keys are written as typed: Enter, Space, `a` ([A]), `e` ([E]), `r` ([R]). VM is « Vorlan Mestrix », ZQ « Zorbal Quentrix », VQ « Vorlène Quentrix ».

The suites that exercise these paths and must stay green: 10.15's `tests/unit/test_cli_corrections_hand_added.py`, `test_validation_workflow.py`, `test_validation_models.py` (if present), `tests/unit/gui/` (the review state and screen), `tests/unit/cli/commands/test_delete_mapping.py`, `test_delete_mapping_type_aware.py`, `tests/unit/test_mapping_repository_delete.py`, `test_type_aware_mapping_repository.py`, `tests/unit/test_erasure_log_no_name.py`, `tests/integration/test_erasure_workflow.py`. Since v1.5 also 10.17's `tests/unit/gui/test_hand_added_every_place.py` (the forms helpers, moved) and 10.20's `tests/unit/test_accents_one_form_cli.py` (composed text, the delete screen). **Two existing test files change** (Task 3.2): `test_delete_mapping_type_aware.py:209` and `:222` (Lionel's Q5b), and `test_accents_one_form_cli.py:112-116` (`SEVERAL_LINE`, Lionel's Q2 over his 10.20 Qc). ### CHANGELOG Entry (Task 5)

`### Fixed`, a new bullet (AC7; the PM may prefer other wording; "saved pseudonyms", never "rows"):

- **Names written in capitals, and deleting a name across capitals (Story 10.19).** In the command line, a name you add with [A] is now also replaced where it is written with other capitals (« ZORBAL », or « Vorlan ZORBAL » for « Vorlan Zorbal »), and in capitals without its accents (« HELENE » for « Hélène »), never in a word written entirely in small letters: adding « Petit » never replaces « petit ». Those places stayed in clear in every earlier version, and the command line could even say that every place was already in the review. A name you correct in the review now also covers its places written in capitals: « ZORBAL QUENTRIX », corrected as « Zorbal Quentrix », was written « [pseudonym] QUENTRIX », already in v2.2.0; in the desktop app's batch review, the correction also reaches the same name written with other capitals, except a place you rejected on its own. In the command-line review, a name written in capitals without its accents (« VORLENE » beside « Vorlène ») is now shown with the name, as one name, so one correction covers both; the desktop app's reviews reach it too. `delete-mapping` with a name now also deletes the saved pseudonyms of the same name written with other capitals or in capitals without its accents, listed with the others before you confirm, each with its own erasure entry; earlier versions kept them. After `delete-mapping --id`, the line about the others left says « mapping » instead of « row » (« 1 other mapping with the same name remains (type: ORG). »). Names that differ only in capitals still get one pseudonym each. Detection, the database schema and the accuracy figures do not change.

**10.7's bullet, aligned (Task 5.3; the PO's N4, the orchestrator's ruling).** `CHANGELOG.md:40`, under `### Changed` in Story 10.7's entry, becomes:

  - **`delete-mapping "Name"`** lists every saved pseudonym of that name (one for each type it was saved under; since Story 10.19, also one for each way it was written with capitals: see "Names written in capitals, and deleting a name across capitals (Story 10.19)" under "Fixed") and deletes all of them, with one erasure audit entry for each. `delete-mapping --id` deletes only one and tells you which others remain for the same name written exactly the same way, and their types. Each erasure audit entry records the id and type of the saved pseudonym deleted, not its name (Story 10.12, under "Fixed").

**10.20's quote, made true again (Task 5.4; the PO's v1.5 M2).** In `CHANGELOG.md:223` (Story 10.20's entry, under `### Fixed`), the words « its screen now reads « You are about to delete the following N saved pseudonyms (all of this name, whatever its type): » » become « its screen now reads « You are about to delete the following N saved pseudonyms (all of this name, whatever its type or its capitals): » », the line 10.19 shows (Lionel's Q2; his 10.20 Qc option said 10.19 adds « or its capitals » back).

### Environment

- **The dev agent and the Codex review (AC9; Lionel, 2026-10-18).** The BMAD dev agent (Claude) writes the tests first (Task 1), then the fix (Task 2) and every later code fix. No other agent writes code on this branch, and no `codex` session that writes (`workspace-write`) runs on it. The orchestrator runs the tests, the lint, the commits and the pushes, and reads every diff before using it. **The final review (Task 6.3)** is one read-only session run by the orchestrator on the PR's final head: `codex exec --sandbox read-only` over `git diff main...HEAD`, with a prompt that asks for correctness, names left in clear (forms, corrections), rows left after an erasure, the small-letter exception, and conformance to this story's Fix Design and approved texts, and ends with a VERDICT line; its session id, model, sandbox and VERDICT line go in the Debug Log. It runs again only if the code changes after it. If `codex` is missing, out of quota or fails, stop and tell Lionel.
- **The measurement-files rule.** `proto_1019.diff`, the trees `base/` and `proto/`, and every file of `sketch/` in `rgpd-analysis/r1019/sm/` are Claude-written measurement files, like the PO's probes (`r1015/po/caps/`, `r1019/po/probe/`, `r1017/po/probe/`) and the PO's `forms_rule.py`. They stay measurement only: never committed, never applied in the worktree. The dev agent writes its own code and tests from this story's Fix Design, Test Plan and AC precisions, plus the isolation rules, and from the repository. The PR body says "code and tests written by the BMAD dev agent (Claude); design and measurement sketches by the SM (Claude); final diff reviewed read-only by `codex exec`" (Task 6.4).
- **The worktree has no toolchain.** Use the main checkout's Poetry environment (`C:\Users\devea\AppData\Local\pypoetry\Cache\virtualenvs\gdpr-pseudonymizer-_kRLwMSQ-py3.11`) through `.bat` files run with `cmd.exe /c`: `cd /d` to a scratch folder (the lint runners `cd` to the worktree), `set PYTHONPATH=C:\Users\devea\Documents\GitHub\rgpd-wt-s1019`, `set QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen`, `set PYTHONUTF8=1`, `set PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, **`set USERPROFILE=C:\Users\devea\Documents\GitHub\rgpd-analysis\r1019\dev\home` and `set HOME=` the same folder** (every runner), then `<env>\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -c <worktree>\pytest.ini --rootdir <worktree> -p no:cacheprovider …`. The pattern is in `rgpd-analysis\r1019\sm\run_check.bat` and `run_suite.bat`; `r1019\sm\plugins\sm_isolation.py` points `gui.config._config_path` at `tmp_path` (`-p`). A `cmd.exe /c` string cannot carry `-m "not slow"` and mangles accented arguments: use `.bat` files and UTF-8 input files. Never `pip install` or `poetry install` from WSL. The full suite builds the docs into `site/` in the worktree (`tests/integration/test_documentation_build.py`, gitignored): remove it after the run.
- Line endings: `.gitattributes:4` is `* text=auto eol=lf`. Commit identity: `git -c user.name="Lionel Deveaux" -c user.email="40073756+LioChanDaYo@users.noreply.github.com" commit …`, with the message ending `Co-Authored-By:` the model that wrote the code; stage files by path, never `-a`/`-A`; never stage `.claude/settings.local.json`.
- Other agents work in the main checkout and the other worktrees (`../rgpd-wt-s1016`, `../rgpd-wt-s1017`, `../rgpd-wt-s1018`, QA's detached worktrees under `rgpd-analysis/`). Never touch them; read another branch only through `git show <commit>:<path>`.
- Scratch work goes in `rgpd-analysis/r1019/`: the dev's in `r1019/dev/`, the SM's in `r1019/sm/`, the PO's in `r1019/po/`, QA's in `r1019/qa/`.

### Project Structure Notes

The rule lives in `validation/models.py`, beside 10.15's `places_of`, so the CLI (`validation/`) and the desktop (`gui/`) can both import it without the CLI importing `gui/` (which needs PySide6). The repository keeps its by-name methods and gains two by-name-any-capitals ones; the CLI command chooses which to call. [Source: architecture/12-unified-project-structure.md] No structural conflict.

### Technical Constraints

- Type hints on every public function; absolute imports; `mypy` strict. [Source: architecture/19-coding-standards.md#19.1]
- Quality commands: `black --check`, `ruff check`, `mypy gdpr_pseudonymizer/`, `pytest`. [Source: architecture/19-coding-standards.md#19.0]
- **Never log sensitive data:** no new log line holds a name; the ERASURE entries stay without a name (10.12). [Source: architecture/19-coding-standards.md#19.1, rule 3]
- No CLI flag, schema or file-format change; the new repository methods are additions.

### Testing

[Source: architecture/16-testing-strategy.md#16.2; pytest.ini; .github/workflows/ci.yaml]

- New tests as in the Test Plan: the real CLI in the test process, the real desktop single-document path.
- Tests first, strict xfail with `raises=AssertionError`, preconditions with `pytest.fail`, proven on every CI leg (AC11).
- Unmarked, spaCy-free, xdist-safe; no wall-clock assert; no sleep.
- Assertions on the written files, the stored rows, the lines shown and the ERASURE entries.
- No test reads or writes the user's settings file.

### Effort

**0.5-1 week** (the epic's estimate since Q3 and Q4): the product change is small (the SM's prototype v1.6 on `097a12c`: 5 files, 222 lines added and 97 removed, most of both the move of 10.17's helpers); the tests (33 items, ten on the desktop) are the larger part, then the hand-over of 10.17's helpers (Task 2.8), the Codex review and QA's end-to-end checks.

### Findings

- **JOIN-001 (high).** In the command line, the forms join can merge another name into a corrected group, through a capitals-without-accents place that [A] put in the accented name's group.
  - « Zelvine » (another name under Q3) is then written with the corrected « Zélvine Testar »'s pseudonym, and its row is not saved.
  - Real output, LOCATION, as above. It fails Task 8's criterion: a text not written as a form at every place never takes the corrected name's pseudonym.
  - A fix direction (the dev's and the SM's to decide): compare a group's own grouping key, or keep [A]'s places with written texts of another small-letter class out of the join; then a guard (QA's probe shape).
- **CAPS-001 (medium).** A correction typed in capitals without accents does not cover the accented places of the same name; `is_form` reaches one way only.
  - In the CLI the review shows them as one name, so this is silent. « Signé : ZELVINE TESTAR. Zélvine Testar a signé. » shows « (2 occurrences, 1 unique) ». [E] typed « ZELVINE TESTAR » (as read at the first place) writes « <p>. <p> Testar a signé. »: « Testar » is in clear.
  - On `main` the two were separate names in the review.
  - In the desktop single document and batch, a correction of the « ZELVINE » row does not reach the « Zélvine » row. That row stays visible, and accepted as it is it leaves « Testar » in clear: Lionel's Q4 case, the other way round.
  - Lionel's Q3 and Q4 speak of the capitals place being a form of the accented name. Whether the reverse is covered is a ruling for the orchestrator, or a question for Lionel.
- **PERF-001 (low).** About 73 ms per correction place not found exactly in a 200 KB document; 7.4 s for 100 capital places. A window-limited search gives the same result.
- **TEST-1019-01 (low).** No committed test has the capitals group first in the document (the « join one way » mutant survives).
- **CI-FLAKY-01 (low).** The macOS timing test above is now flaky.
- **DOC-1019-01 (low).** The PR body and the Codex VERDICT are not there yet (orchestrator).
- **Info:**
  - **An erasure under an accentless name.** `delete-mapping Zelvine` (or « Quovome Zorbal ») also deletes a saved « ZÉLVINE » (or « QUOVÔME ZORBAL »): a name entirely in capitals is a form of both spellings, as AC4 reads. The screen lists it before the confirmation; with `--force` it is named only in the success lines.
  - **Three mutants are equivalent:** the join that chains, the delete without the small-letter branch, and the fallback without the window.

### The SM's class checks, and QA's own (item 4)

The SM's four scripts were run unchanged on the head:

| Check | Head `f8c28a6` | `fa9f2ac` |
|---|---|---|
| `class_join.py`, 3000 documents | 0 documents join non-forms (994 join) | 318 |
| `class_join.py`, Codex's three documents | « MEZOR » apart from both families, in every order | one group |
| `class_delete.py`, 28 rows of 6 names | 0 missed, 0 rows of other people touched | 5 missed |
| `class_caps.py`, 4834 corrections of 670 joined groups | 0 places written with a partial span, joined or own; 5076 sent back | 2463 and 2613 |
| `class_unjoined.py`, 47,634 pairs against `main` | the 28,770 unreached pairs all keep `main`'s span; 6,952 reached only by a form | (SM: the same) |

**QA's own, `class_e2e19.py`** (aimed at what those four do not cover: the real CLI end to end, with groups sent back and the written file):
- **The setup:** 400 documents, with fold-equal names (« Zélvine »/« Zelvine », « Mézor »/« Mèzor », « Quentrèl »/« Quentrel »). Each place is written in title case or in capitals with accents kept or dropped. The detector finds a whole name, its first word or its last word. Every group shown, sent-back ones included, is corrected with the person's usual name or with the name as written there.
- **The result:** 1,759 places and 1,137 groups shown. At the head, no place is in clear and no place carries another person's pseudonym where `main` does not do the same.
  - The head's 99 places in clear and 16 wrong ones are all shared with `main`: a variant group confirmed where the detector found one word, or one found text that stands for two people. `main` has 370 and 24.
  - The head fixes 16 places in clear and 9 wrong ones that `fa9f2ac` had.
  - One place differs the other way. It is a flow difference: at `fa9f2ac` the join changed which place the group showed, and the head does what `main` does.
- **What none of the class checks covers:** the desktop paths and old rows with another accent. That is where the three remaining cases are (Findings).

### Findings

- **Resolved:**
  - JOIN-001, CAPS-001 in the command line, PERF-001 and TEST-1019-01 (`c341244`, `750937c`);
  - Codex's first-review P0-1 and P0-2, as Task 9 ruled them.
- **DEL-1019-01 (high), new.** `delete-mapping` misses an old damaged row of a capitals form whose accents differ from the typed name's. This is Codex's second-review P0-3, reproduced by QA.
  - **The rows:** QA's own, written by v2.2.0 and by `43041e9` from documents whose accents are stored as separate marks:
    - « VORLAN MÈLORA », saved as « VORLAN » + space + lone U+0300 + « LORA »;
    - likewise « MÈLORA ZORBAL », « ZÈLVINE MÈZOR » and « À VORLAN MÈLORA » (LOCATION).
  - **The command:** `delete-mapping "Vorlan Mélora"`, or « VORLAN MÉLORA ».
    - The old row stays.
    - With today's row of the same text saved too, the command erases today's row, writes one ERASURE entry, says the pseudonym « is now permanently anonymous » and exits 0.
    - Without today's row, it answers « not found » (exit 1).
    - Typed as written in the document (« VORLAN MÈLORA »), both rows go.
  - **Why it counts:** under Lionel's Q3 and AC5 (« HELÈNE » is a form of « Hélène »), the capitals text is a form of the name, and today's row of it is erased.
  - **The cause:** the old forms are built from the typed name's own accents. The old row's lone mark comes from the document's other accent, so the letters filter skips the row.
  - **A second shape, found by QA's own probe:** the name typed without its accents (« Quovome Zorbal », « Belora Melora ») erases today's « QUOVÔME ZORBAL » or « BELORA MÉLORA », but not the old row of the same text (20 of 72 cases).
  - **Count:** 32 of 48 runs at the head.
  - **A fix direction (the dev's and the SM's to decide):**
    - for a saved row written entirely in capitals with a lone mark, set accents and lone marks aside on both sides (Q3 does this for capitals only);
    - keep the sign rule;
    - add guards of QA's shapes, with today's row saved too and with other people's rows next to them.
- **CAPS-002 (medium), new.** Desktop batch review, « Signé : ZÉLVINE TESTAR. Zélvine Testar a signé. ». This is Codex's second-review P0-2, reproduced by QA.
  - Correcting the « ZÉLVINE » row as « ZELVINE TESTAR » reaches the « Zélvine » row through Q4.
  - The correction is not found there, yet the row is marked modified with its old span: « Signé : <p>. <p> Testar a signé. ». Nothing is left pending, so this is silent.
  - `main` keeps that row pending. G10 covers only a source row without the accent.
  - In single-document review and in the CLI the two places are one group that was not joined, and they write what `main` writes (ruled).
- **CAPS-003 (medium), new.** Desktop, single document and batch, « Vorlène Quentrix Zorbal a signé. Signé : VORLENE QUENTRIX ZORBAL. ». This is Codex's second-review P0-1, reproduced by QA.
  - « Vorlène » corrected to « Vorlène Quentrix » also modifies « VORLENE ».
  - A second correction, to « Vorlène Quentrix Zorbal », no longer reaches it: the every-place check reads its modified span « VORLENE QUENTRIX ».
  - The file reads « <p1> a signé. Signé : <p2> ZORBAL. », silently.
  - `main` leaves « VORLENE » pending.
- **The three as a group:** all three are in Task 9's classes (P0-2's erasure, CAPS-001's ruling) on paths Task 9 did not reach. None of the SM's class checks, nor QA's, covered them: they cover the CLI and the accent-dropped rows.
- **New, TEST-1019-02 and TEST-1019-03 (low):** two test gaps that QA's mutants show (item 7). Nothing pins the delete branch for a row with no mark whose form keeps every word, and nothing has a capitals form that starts before the found place. Both are tests only, and the head behaves correctly in both.
- **In the backlog:** CI-FLAKY-01. DOC-1019-01 is the coordinator's.
- **Info (OBS-1019-02):**
  - the desktop search does not list the old « BELORA » + lone U+0301 + « LORA » row for « Bélora Mélora », as on `main` (AC12);
  - the CLI's closing panel still says « Reviewed 1 unique entities » after a place came back;
  - a variant group that was not joined still writes « <p> Testar » as `main` does (ruled);
  - `delete-mapping` on 10,000 rows takes 0.33–0.34 s.
- **Before the merge:** a fix of DEL-1019-01, CAPS-002 and CAPS-003 with guards (or the orchestrator's ruling on the two mediums), then QA's re-check and a new read-only Codex review.

### Findings

- **Resolved:** DEL-1019-01, CAPS-002, CAPS-003, TEST-1019-02 (G12), TEST-1019-03 (G13), and Lionel's leak at a group's own places (for spaces and tabs).

  - **The rows:** QA's rows written by v2.2.0 and by `43041e9` from title-case names with another accent:
    - « Vorlan Mèlora », saved as « Vorlan » + space + lone U+0300 + « lora »;
    - « Zélvine Mèzor »;
    - « Mèlora Zorbal ».
  - **The command:** `delete-mapping "Vorlan Mélora"` (or « VORLAN MÉLORA ») erases that row. « Zélvine Mézor » and « Mélora Zorbal » do the same, and so does « Vorlan Mèlora » for the row of « Vorlan Mélora ».
  - **Why it is wrong:** under Q3 a title-case name with another accent is another name. Today's row of that same text is kept, while its old row goes.
  - **`f8c28a6` and `main`** keep these rows.
  - **Count:** 20 runs (5 shapes, 2 old versions, with and without today's row).
  - **The cause:** `_one_mark` makes every mark the same in the old-forms branch, for title-case rows too. The ruling sets accents aside in capitals only.
  - **A fix direction** (measured as mutant OM4): keep the marks as they are in the old-forms branch, and `_one_mark` in the capitals-only seeds branch. Every committed test, QA probe and SM delete check passes with it.
  - The third Codex review reports this too (its P0-3).
- **DEL-1019-03 (high), new.** A hyphenated name typed without its accents misses its old capitals row. This is the third Codex review's P0-4, reproduced by QA.
  - **The row:** QA's v2.2.0 and `43041e9` rows from « BELORA-MÉLORA QUOVÔME ZORBAL », saved as « BELORA- » + lone U+0301 + « LORA QUOVO » + U+0302 + « ZORBAL ».
  - **The command:** `delete-mapping "Belora-Melora Quovome Zorbal"` erases today's capitals row and says the pseudonym is now permanently anonymous, but the old row stays.
  - **The cause:** `_damage_seeds` finds no « ME » cut after a hyphen.
  - **10.20's own gap, recorded for routing:** for the two-word « Bélora-Mélora Zorbal », 10.20's saved forms hold no hyphen cut at all.
    - So the exact name misses v2.2.0's title-case row (« not found », on `main` too).
    - With today's « BELORA-MÉLORA ZORBAL » saved, the head erases that row and reports success while the old one stays.
- **WIDEN-1019-01 (medium), new.** The widening runs over another person's surname. QA's own probe found it, and so does the third Codex review (its P0-1).
  - **The case:** « Zélvine Testar a signé. Zélvine Tèstar valide. » (and Codex's « Mézor »/« Mèzor »), « Zélvine » found, [E] « Zélvine Testar ».
  - **The result:** « Zélvine Tèstar », another name under Q3, is written whole with the corrected name's pseudonym, in the CLI and the desktop. `main` writes « <p> Tèstar ».
  - **The cause:** the added words are compared with their accents set aside, even in title case.
  - **The trade-off, for the orchestrator:** comparing them in Q3's direction (the written word a form of the typed one) ends the merge. But it leaves « Tèstar » in clear when « ZELVINE TESTAR » is typed for « Zélvine Tèstar ».
- **CAPS-004 (medium), new.** A second correction made from the capitals row leaves « Zorbal » in clear. This is the third Codex review's P0-2, reproduced through the real screen's dialog, single and batch.
  - **The steps:** « Vorlène » is corrected to « Vorlène Quentrix » (both rows take it), then the « VORLENE » row is corrected to « Vorlène Quentrix Zorbal ».
  - **The result:** « <p1> Zorbal a signé. Signé : <p2>. », with no row pending. CAPS-003's fix works only from the row that was corrected first.
  - `main` leaves more in clear here (« <p1> Zorbal … <p2> QUENTRIX ZORBAL »).
- **LEAK-1019-01 (medium), new.** Lionel's leak stays across a line break. QA's own probe and the third Codex review (its P0-5).

### The place's « A » at the row's end (item 6)

**QA's judgement: safe.**
- **Why it can't reach another name:** the « A » is read only after a space and only as the row's last word, and only for a place's row, so the letters before it must still be the typed name's. A real French place name does not end with a separate word « A ».
- **QA's comparison:** `headpkg9p`, the head with only « MAITRE » offered there (the SM's prototype), gives byte-identical output on the wide set and the v1.21 set.
- **QA's other-place probes** (« Élora », « Lora », « Mélor », « Saint-Mélora », « Saint-Lora », « Saint-Élora » against « MÉLORA À » and « SAINT-MÉLORA À ») erase nothing.
- **It is also untested:** the mutant that drops it (N4) survives every committed test and QA's probes. It changes nothing QA could measure.
- **v2.2.0's own rows of a place ending « À »** keep the grave mark (lone U+0300 after the « A »), so neither choice reaches them. This is the same on every head, and it needs a place added by hand ending with « À » (exotic).

- **The tree:** `wt_8e0e632`, whose product code is `c2379c4`'s.
- **With `--runxfail`:** the three fail at the dev's lines, `:1577` (T47), `:1615` (T48) and `:1653` (T49), each an `AssertionError` with no « PRE: ».

### Findings

- **Resolved:** DEL-1019-09 (`40588e4`) and TEST-1019-08 (G30, `8e0e632`).
- **DEL-1019-10 (medium), new; not a regression.** The ninth Codex review's P0, reproduced on QA's v2.2.0 rows (`test_q19_r9p.py`, on the head, `c2379c4`, `63da306`, `6d87d22` and `main`).
  - **The rows:** v2.2.0 saves « MÉLORA (MÉLIME) TESTAR » as a lone U+0301 + « LORA ( » + a lone U+0301 + « LIME) TESTAR ». The same happens for « QUOVANE (MÉLORA) », « MÉLORA [MÉLIME] TESTAR » and « MÉLORA/MÉLIME TESTAR ».
  - **What misses:** the name typed without accents misses the old row while today's row goes and the name is reported anonymous. For « QUOVANE (MÉLORA) », the accented name misses it too. 10 of 20 spellings miss on every head since `6d87d22`; `main` misses 16.
  - **What works:** « QUOVANE TESTAR (NÉE MÉLORA) » is erased in every spelling, because there the cut follows a space.
  - **The cause:** `bound_before` accepts a cut after a space, a mark or the name's separators, but not after « ( », « [ » or « / ».
  - **Real French documents:** « NOM (PRÉNOM) » lists are common and given names starting « Mé » are common (« Mélanie »). The old row needs the bracketed text added by hand as one name in v2.2.0. QA reads it as rare but realistic.
  - **Grade:** medium, under Lionel's rule. If the orchestrator rules it exotic, low, and the gate PASS 100.
- **OBS-1019-06 (info), new; not a regression.** A capitals title lost whole leaves an old row with no mark: v2.2.0 saves « MME VORLAN » as « VORLAN » and « DR LIME » as « LIME ».
  - `delete-mapping "Mme Vorlan"` and `"Dr Lime"` keep these rows on every head and on `main`. « Vorlan » and « Lime » erase them.
  - This is the wide set's 502 rows "typed with a title word".
  - **Real French documents:** common (« MME VORLAN » in capitals).
  - **Why it is not simply a miss:** a bare surname row may be another person's (« M. Vorlan »), so reaching it from a name typed with a title is a PO decision, not a miss to fix here.
- **Backlog, unchanged:** the « J.-M. » save quirk, the two-word hyphen gap, the closing panel's count, CI-FLAKY-01. DOC-1019-01 is the coordinator's.
- **Before the merge:** the orchestrator rules on DEL-1019-10, and the ninth Codex review asks for changes (AC9).
